0x1f50f func wl3_PrepareFile hand (data) byte-swaps a freshly loaded big-endian file in place (called by level_LoadWorld and path_LoadFile before world_CreateInstance). Magic "PATH" (DAT_000a0e30) -> path_PrepareFile. Else .wl3: bswap32 header +0 (object offset table offset), +4 (hierarchy offset), +8 (sub-block offset), +0xc (u16 table offset: be16 count + count u16), +0x10 object count, +0x14, +0x18 total object RAM; matrix +0x1c..+0x3c; pos +0x40..+0x48; wl3_SwapSubBlock; object offset table (count entries at data+data[0]); wl3_SwapChunk(data+0x5c) world chunk list. Hierarchy at data+data[1]: 0x14-byte nodes (u16 +0, u16 +2, dwords +4..+0x10) walked with the pointer stack DAT_000bf7f8 (limit DAT_000bf7f4+8 -> sys_Fatal outofstack): node short +0 > 0 pushes (child count, index) so subtrees are visited. Then epilog_15511. Same code in LORT 0x060f9b48 (the Saturn runs it as a no-op check)
0x1f010 func be16_read hand (p) returns the big-endian u16 at p; used by wl3_PrepareFile for the stream counts
0x1f3f2 func wl3_SwapSubBlock hand (data) swaps the sub-block at data + data[+8] if present: 4 dwords, then from +0x10 a tree of records walked with the pointer stack DAT_000bf7f8 (0 sentinel pushed first): first byte < 1 -> leaf (octree_SwapNode16, then its count entries: be16 word, low byte bits 0..5 = n, bit 6 clear = n inline bytes, p = (p+n+3) & ~1, set = n shorts swapped), else branch (octree_SwapNode32, 8 child links); pops the next record until the sentinel. Children are reached only through the pushed links, so the block is a tree (octree-like, 8 links per branch) not a flat list

0x1f31d func octree_SwapNode16 hand (rec) swaps a node16 of the .wl3 +8 octree (the node type that carries face lists): header word unswapped (byte 0: bit 7 set or 0 = node16, bits 0..6 = entry count; byte 1 = 8-bit child mask); for each set mask bit (bit 7 first) a be16 child offset in shorts, relative to the position after it, is swapped and the child pushed on DAT_000bf7f8; returns the pointer past the links (the caller swaps the entries: be16 header, bits 15..7 object index, bit 6 = 16-bit face indices, bits 5..0 face count; payload bytes padded to even or shorts swapped). 0x1f38d func octree_SwapNode32 hand (rec) swaps a node32 of the .wl3 +8 octree (first byte 1..0x7f, no face lists): 8 child slots: slot 0 = be16 at +2 (the first short stays the type byte pair), slots 1..7 be32 (the swapper keeps the low 24 bits for the push, the runtime uses the whole dword); nonzero offsets, relative to the position after the slot, are pushed on DAT_000bf7f8. 0x15511 func epilog_15511 hand NOT a function: shared epilogue (pop ebp edi esi edx ecx ebx; ret), the tail-jump target of every loader (wl3_PrepareFile, path_PrepareFile, track_SegDistance, level_LoadSetup, octree_SwapNode16 ...); 0x15512 is the entry without ebp (octree_SwapNode32). 0x1212e2 func octree_CollectFaces hand the runtime reader of the .wl3 +8 tree, called by col_SegVsMesh after it set the query box (segment AABB +- radius in the locals and DAT_00120040 = &segment, DAT_0012003c = continuation): root = wl3 + wl3[8]: 4 dwords x, y, z, size (cube), nodes from +0x10. Node32 (first byte 1..0x7f): child k at ptr + 4k + 4 + slot k (slot 0 is the u16 at +2) pushed with the child origin and half size. 0x1214cb func octree_PushNode16Children hand (half, x?, y?, z?) for a node16 record: byte 1 = child mask; for each set bit (0x80 first) consumes one u16 offset in shorts (child = position after the offset + offset*2) and pushes it with the octant origin and the half size when the query octant mask (EDI) also has the bit. 0x120e1a func col_MeshTestFaces hand continuation of col_SegVsMesh after octree_CollectFaces: for every touched object (DAT_00120028..DAT_0012002c) walks its collected face lists (ObjRAMHeader +8, cleared), clears face bit 1, converts the segment into object space (obj record +0x28 scale: inverse = 2^32 / (scale<<2); position +0x2c..+0x34; DAT_00120200..0x214 endpoints, DAT_00120040[8] radius), FUN_00121049; per face: 4-byte table at obj+obj[2] (+8): signed bytes cx, ?, cz, r = XZ bounding circle test against the segment midpoint DAT_00120040[0x19]/[0x1b] radius [0x1c]; index stream obj+obj[0]: u16 n1 then n1 triangles (3 u16) then u16 n2 and n2 quads (4 u16), faces 0..n1-1 are the triangles; vertex indices are premultiplied by 2 (vertex = obj+obj[1] + index*3 = 3 shorts x y z); face flags u16 at obj+obj[5] + face*2 -> DAT_0012010e; the face is copied into the polygon record DAT_00120104 (+0xa flags, +0xc vertex count, verts from +0x138 down, +0x10 radius) and FUN_001212a1 (segment vs polygon) is run; flag 0x10 = quad split into two triangles (second pass with flags 0xf, 3 verts). 0x123103 func col_PolySetup hand polygon record prep: +1 = 0, edge vectors +0x54 = v1-v2 and +0x3c = v2-v3 (verts at +0x120 +0x12c +0x138, +0x114 for quads), segment dir +0xb4 = end +0x108 - start +0xfc, face normal +0xd8 = normalize(cross) to the fixed length PTR_FUN_001200b8 (x87 sqrt)
0x122964 func col_SphereVsPlane hand sphere sweep vs the polygon plane: d = (start - v3)*n >> 16 (+0x168), dir*n (+0x16c) >= 0 -> 0 (back-facing or moving away); 0 <= d <= radius (+0x10) -> type 4 already touching, t = 0; d < -radius -> 0; t = (d - r) / -(dir*n) (64-bit), t < 0 -> 0, t > best t +0x20 -> 1 (farther than the current hit); contact +0x24 = start + dir*t, plane point +0x30 = contact - n*r; col_PointInPoly == 0 -> 0; else best t = t, saved point +0x144, plane point +0x150, normal +0x15c, +1 = 1, +0 = type, returns 1
0x122f4a func col_PointInPoly hand 2-D point in polygon: drops the dominant normal axis (|n| max of +0xd8..+0xe0 picks the two offsets 0/4/8), then edge cross products v(i)-v(i+1) x (p - v(i+1)) must all share the sign of the polygon's own winding (3 or 4 vertices by +0xc); p = plane point +0x30. 0x123239 func col_PolyEdgeSetup hand before the edge tests: extra edge vectors (+0x60/+0x6c for quads, +0x48 for triangles: v0-v3), unit direction +0xc0 = normalize(dir +0xb4) (x87), and the four edge flags +2..+5 = 1 (= not yet hit)

0x1225ef func col_SolveSweepQuadratic hand (EDI -> 64-bit coefficients c, b, a; EBP+0x10 radius) smallest root t in 0..best t (+0x20) of a*t^2 + b*t + c = 0 via col_EvalQuadratic64 and a 64-bit sqrt; c <= 0 (already overlapping) -> t = 0 contact = start (+0x24 = +0xfc), returns 1; writes t to +0x1c, +6 = solved flag

0x15d190 func blit_Gouraud8 hand (rec in EAX) texture depack blitter, 8bpp out, table DAT_000c0a74 entry +4: rec = tex_DepackOne record ([0] dest, [1] A pixels, [2] A palette u16, [3] wA, [4] hA, [5] B pixels = 2x2 u16 RGB555 corner colours, [0xc] GRDMUL, [0xd] cmp table, [0xe] ctx+0x80); patches (rec[0xe]+0x48 = ctx+0xc8 budget) into the budget sub at 0x15d3c0; if DAT_00155e80 (suspended) restores the 0x29 saved dwords DAT_00156688 and resumes; else builds a 0x80-byte frame: the 4 corner colours split into 3 channels each as 16.16 ((c<<6 & 0x1f0000) + 0x8000 for r, g, b), flags rec[0xb] 1 flip x (negate x step, start at the right) 2 flip y 4 transpose (swap w/h and steps), per-row channel steps = (bottom-top)*4 * recip[h] (0x125b7c), per-pixel steps = (right-left)*4 * recip[w]; then blit_Gouraud8Loop. Per pixel: A texel -> palette u16 (0xffff = transparent -> 0) -> each 5-bit channel times the interpolated 5-bit light through the 32x32 product table rec[0xc] -> 15-bit -> patched cmp table -> 8-bit. The budget (rows) is charged per row; when it runs out the state is saved to DAT_00156688, DAT_00155e80 = 1, returns 1

0x15d890 func blit_Mul8 hand (rec) texture depack blitter, 8bpp out, table entry +0xc: A texel x B map: B is a 4-bit map (nibble per pixel, [7]/[8] size) whose nibble selects an RGB555 colour from the B palette rec[6]; both colours spread to 3x10-bit lanes (x*0x8001 & 0x1f07c1f), combined ((B<<5)|A per lane) and multiplied lane by lane through the 1024-entry product table DAT_00155e84, repacked to 15-bit, then the cmp table -> 8-bit; A palette 0xffff = transparent (0). Self-modifying: rec fields patched into the loop (0x15d9d7 src, 0x15da18 dest, 0x15dab5 cmp, 0x15da3c/0x15da52 B pixels/palette, ...); u/v start 1000 (0x3e8 = 16.16 1000/65536 = 0.015 texel) and span 0x59dfe0c0*recip (step over the whole source), flips via rec[0xb] bits 1/2 (start 0x1677fc18 and negated step); budget rec[0xe]+0x48 charged per row, suspend/restore through DAT_00155e80 / DAT_00156688 (12 dwords)
